Implementation details for softmax objective and gradient:
```
def softmax_scores(v, beta):
    # v float array, center by max for numerical
    m = v.max(); w = exp(v-m); p = w/sum(w)
    c = fft_conv(p,p)
    h = n*c
    hmax = h.max()
    scaled = beta*(h-hmax)
    exps = exp(scaled)
    L = hmax + log(exps.sum())/beta
    q = exps/exps.sum()
    score = 2*L
    g_p = 2 * cross_corr(p,q)  # derivative of max h? h=n*c, derivative n*(2 conv?) -> 2n? Let's derive.
```
If objective L_h = approx max h = max (n c) = n * approx max c. Gradient wrt p of approx max c is q * (T_k+T_k^T) p? For c, with weights q_t (over t), ∂(∑q_t c_t)/∂p_j = 2∑q_t p_{t-j} = 2 cross. Thus ∂(n ∑q_t c_t)/∂p = 2n cross. The LSE L_h = n*L_c (since L_h ≈ max n c = n max c). So g_p = n * g_c where g_c=2 cross. So `g_p = 2.0 * n * conv_res` where conv_res=∑ p_i q_{j+i}? We can compute with FFT length enough.
Then grad_v = p * (g_p - dot(p,g_p)).
```
Need compute cross truncation: q length 2n-1. p length n. cross[j] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i q_{j+i}. This is same as rev_p conv q at [n-1:2n-1]. Use FFT length M2 >= 3n-2 as previous.

Softmax gradient: ∂p_j/∂v_k = p_j(δ_jk - p_k). So grad_v_k = ∑_j g_p_j p_j(δ_jk - p_k) = p_k(∑_j g_p_j p_j - ∑_j g_p_j p_j? Wait gradient of f(p) wrt v: g_v_k = ∑_j ∂f/∂p_j ∂p_j/∂v_k = ∑_j g_p_j p_j (δ_{jk} - p_k) = p_k ∑_j g_p_j - p_k ∑_j g_p_j p_j? Actually ∂f/∂p_j = g_p_j. Then ∑_j g_p_j p_j δ_{jk} = p_k g_p_k. Then -p_k ∑_j g_p_j p_j. So grad_v = p*(g_p - mean_g) where mean_g = dot(p,g_p). Yes.

This enforces tangent to simplex. L-BFGS on v can move. Need objective returns (score, grad_v). Good.

```
def conv_full_cheb(a, n):
    M = 2*n -1
    M_fft = 1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M_fft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M_fft)[:M]
    # remove tiny negative due to FFT
    # c should nonnegative for nonnegative p, but numerical may negative; clip small.
    return c
```
For p positive, c positive. Could clip negative to 0 for score; but gradient uses p.

```
def lse_v_objective(v, beta):
    m=v.max(); ew=np.exp(v-m); p=ew/ew.sum()
    c=fft_conv(p)
    h=len(c)/? n = len(v); h = n*c
    hmax = h.max()
    scaled = beta*(h-hmax)
    exps = np.exp(np.clip(scaled, -50, 0)? scaled <=0 because hmax max, but numerical maybe >0 small; clip to 0 maybe)
    L = hmax + np.log(exps.sum())/beta
    q = exps/exps.sum()
    score = 2*L
    # cross
    g_p = 2.0*n*cross_corr(p,q)
    mean_g = float(p.dot(g_p))
    grad_v = p*(g_p - mean_g)
    return score, grad_v
```

`cross_corr(p,q)`:
```
def cross_corr(p,q):
    n=len(p); L=len(q)
    M=2*n+L-1? q length 2n-1, p length n, conv length 3n-2. Need output indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n.
    M_fft=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    rev = p[::-1]
    P = rfft(rev, M_fft); Q=rfft(q, M_fft)
    conv = irfft(P*Q, M_fft)[:3*n-2]
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Let i'=n-1-i -> =∑_{i'} p_{i'} q_{k-(n-1-i')} =∑ p_{i'} q_{k-n+1+i'}. We want q_{j+i'}, so k-n+1 = j => k=j+n-1. Thus out[j]=conv[j+n-1], for j=0..n-1 => indices n-1..2n-2. Good.

`lse_u_objective(u, beta)`:
- p = softmax_u(u,n)
- c = fft_conv(p)
- h = n*c
- hmax = c.max()*n
- scaled = beta*(h - hmax); clip to [-50,0] perhaps because scaled<=0, but due to round error small positive; clip upper 0.
- exp_s = exp(scaled); sum_e; L = hmax + log(sum_e)/beta
- q = exp_s / sum_e
- score=2*L
- cross = cross_corr(p,q)
- g_p = 2.0*n*cross
- mean_g = p @ g_p
- grad_v = p*(g_p - mean_g)
- grad_u = grad_v[1:]
- return score, grad_u
```
Need n inside; we can store `n = len(u)+1`.

def softmax_u(u, ref_idx):
    n = len(u)+1
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v[:] = 0.0
    # fill indices before/after ref
    if ref_idx > 0:
       v[:ref_idx] = u[:ref_idx]
    if ref_idx < n-1:
       v[ref_idx+1:] = u[ref_idx:]
    m = v.max()  # maybe 0
    e = np.exp(v - m)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0: ...
    return e/s
```

def multiplicative_line_search(p, beta, max_iters, time_limit):
   t0=time.monotonic(); best=p.copy(); best_sc=internal_score(p); cur=p.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
   eta=0.05

      c=fast_conv(cur); h=n*c; hmax=h.max()
      scaled=beta*(h-hmax); exps=...; w=exps/sum; q=w

         cur=p_new; cur_sc=sc; eta *= 1.05
      else:
         eta *= 0.5

      if sc<best_sc: best=p_new; best_sc=sc
      if time exceeded break
   return best,best_sc
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
But if m=0? For n=1. Use `m=max(1,int(m))`.

`fft_conv(a)`:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = 2*n - 1
    if m <= 0: return np.zeros(0)
    mf = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, mf)[:m]
    # clip tiny negatives
    # due to p small, errors ~1e-18
    c[c < 0] = 0.0
    return c
```
Clipping all negatives to 0 may alter gradient? only score. In gradient q uses h from p; if a negative due to error negligible. Good.

`cross_corr(p,q)`:
```
def cross_corr(p,q):
    n = len(p)
    m = len(q)
    total = n + m - 1  # 3n-2
    mf = 1 << (total-1).bit_length()
    rev = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rev, mf)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, mf)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(R*Q, mf)[:total]
    return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```

```

`multiplicative_descent(p, time_limit, label)`:

```
def multiplicative_descent(p, time_limit, betas=(1000,10000,100000), max_eta=...):
    t0=time.monotonic(); cur=normalize(p); best=cur; cur_sc=internal_score(cur); best_sc=cur_sc
    for beta in betas:
       iters = max(50, int((time_limit - elapsed)*20?))? 
       eta=0.02
       for it in range(...):
          if time exceeded break
          c=fft_conv(cur); h=n*c; hmax=h.max(); cur_sc=2*n*hmax
          scaled=beta*(h-hmax); exps...; q
          cross=cross_corr(cur,q); g=2*n*cross
          # clip g maybe
          p_new = cur / (1.0 + eta*g)
          p_new=normalize(p_new)
          c2=fft_conv(p_new); sc2=2*n*np.max(c2)
          if sc2 < cur_sc - 1e-12:
              cur=p_new; cur_sc=sc2; eta *= 1.05
              if cur_sc<best_sc: best=...
          else:
              eta *= 0.5
              if eta < 1e-5: break
       # if no improvement many iters, next beta
    return best,best_sc
```

Upsampling function:
```
def resample_p(p, n_new):
    n=len(p)
    if n==n_new: return p
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_p(q)
```
For n=7000 to 8700 okay.

Let's detail `run_softmax_continuation` with measured t_obj.
Pseudo code:
```
def run_softmax_continuation(p0, beta_schedule, time_limit, label=""):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    p=normalize_p(p0)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=internal_score(p)
    print(f"[{label}] start {best_sc:.8f}")
    if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: return best_p,best_sc
    # measure t_obj
    u, ref = init_u_ref(p)
    t1=time.monotonic()
    val, grad = lse_u_objective(u, 100.0, ref)
    t_obj = max(time.monotonic()-t1, 1e-6)
    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: break
        remaining_total = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
        # give each beta at most min(600, remaining/(len-bi)) but at least 20
        beta_limit = min(600.0, max(30.0, remaining_total / max(1, len(beta_schedule)-bi)))
        beta_end = time.monotonic() + beta_limit
        # reinitialize from current best or current? Use current_p.
        p_cur = p.copy()
        u, ref = init_u_ref(p_cur)
        # chunks
        while time.monotonic() < beta_end:
            remaining = beta_end - time.monotonic()
            if remaining <= 0: break
            maxfun = max(100, min(1000, int(2.0 / t_obj)))
            # expected time = maxfun*t_obj ~ <=2s if t_obj small; if t_obj large maxfun lower.
            t_chunk=time.monotonic()
            try:
                res = minimize(lse_u_objective, u, args=(beta, ref), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                               options={'maxiter': max(50, maxfun//2), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8})
            except Exception:
                break
            elapsed_chunk = time.monotonic()-t_chunk
            if res.nfev>1:
                t_obj = 0.5*t_obj + 0.5*(elapsed_chunk/float(res.nfev))
            u_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(u_new)): break
            u_new = np.clip(u_new, -60.0, 60.0)
            # check if any substantial progress? if res.fun not finite break
            if not np.isfinite(res.fun): break
            p_opt = softmax_u(u_new, ref)
            p_opt = normalize_p(p_opt)
            sc_opt = internal_score(p_opt)
            # exact score may worsen but continue based on res.fun? For accepting cur, compare to previous p_cur internal? We need p_cur_sc.
            # Keep a variable u_cur? For continuation, maybe accept if sc_opt < sc_cur + 0.05 or beta high? Let's store sc_cur.
```

Code for `multiplicative_descent`:
```
def multiplicative_descent(p0, time_limit, label=""):
    n=len(p0)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    p=normalize_p(p0); best=p.copy(); best_sc=internal_score(p); cur_sc=best_sc
    eta=0.05
    no_improve=0
    for beta in [1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0]:
        it=0
        while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
            it+=1
            c=fft_conv(p)
            h=n*c
            hmax=float(np.max(h))
            cur_sc=2.0*hmax
            # weights
            scaled=beta*h - beta*hmax
            scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
            e=np.exp(scaled)
            s=e.sum()
            q=e/s
            cross=cross_corr(p,q)
            g=2.0*n*cross
            # scale eta by current score-lb maybe
            p_new = p / (1.0 + eta*g)
            p_new=normalize_p(p_new)
            c_new=fft_conv(p_new)
            sc_new=2.0*n*float(np.max(c_new))
            if sc_new < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                p=p_new; cur_sc=sc_new
                if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur_sc
                    print
                eta *= 1.05
                no_improve=0
            else:
                eta *= 0.5
                no_improve +=1
                if eta < 1e-6 or no_improve > 50:
                    eta = min(0.1, max(eta*2, 1e-4))? maybe break beta
                    if no_improve>100: break
        if best_sc <= 1.001... break? no
    return best,best_sc
```
Need if `g` all zeros, p_new=p, no improve forever. Break.

Maybe use multiple beta in ascending; if high beta exact, good.

Alternative use **subgradient mirror ascent with adaptive eta**: eta = (cur_sc - lb) / (g @ g?) But p_new using g. We can set eta from Polyak: objective \(φ(p)=2n max h\). Subgradient in log? Simpler backtracking.

def refine_with_transforms(best_p, time_left):
   p=best_p.copy(); sc=internal_score(p)
   transforms=[]
   # reversed
   transforms.append(p[::-1])
   # power 0.8, 1.2
   transforms.append(normalize(np.power(p+1e-30,0.8)))
   transforms.append(normalize(np.power(p+1e-30,1.2)))
   # smoothed via FFT lowpass
   A=np.fft.rfft(p); freqs; att=1-0.05*(freqs/len(A))**2; transforms.append(normalize(irfft(A*att)[:len(p)]))
   for alt in transforms:
      base,best_sc = combine_candidates(p, alt, [0.1,0.3,0.5])
      if best_sc < sc:
          # maybe multip short
          p2,sc2 = multiplicative_descent(base, min(20,time_left...))
```

Now, potential bug: `np.fft.irfft(A * A, mf)` for real convolution: Because A is rfft of real a, `A*A` is not necessarily Hermitian? A*A is not Hermitian? rfft returns complex; product of a signal with itself in frequency (not conjugate) corresponds to circular convolution? Wait! Important: For linear convolution of a with a (not autocorrelation), using FFT: `fft(a)*fft(a)` gives circular convolution if length >=2n-1, yes because convolution theorem: DFT of circular convolution = product of DFTs. The result is real if a real? The product of DFTs of a real signal is not necessarily conjugate symmetric? Let's check: A[-ω] = conj(A[ω]). Product A[ω]^2. For negative freq, A[-ω]^2 = (conj A[ω])^2 = conj(A[ω]^2). So it is conjugate symmetric. Good, irfft real. This is convolution a*a. For autocorrelation, use A*conj(A). Good.

Cross-correlation gradient: We need conv rev_p * q. rev_p and q real nonnegative. Product R*Q with R=fft(rev_p), Q=fft(q). For linear convolution, product. Is result real? R[-ω]=conj(R[ω]) (rev real), Q[-ω]=conj(Q[ω]), product conjugate symmetric. Good.

But in `fast_conv`, we do `A*A`, not `A*conj(A)`. Good.

Now, the derivative of c=a*a: We used cross_conv rev_p*q where q weights over t. Need verify with small example to ensure gradient matches finite difference. Take n=2, p=[a,b]. c=[a^2,2ab,b^2]. ∂ max? Suppose q over c. ∂(q0 c0 + q1 c1 + q2 c2)/∂a =2a q0 +2b q1. Cross formula: rev=[b,a]; q length3. conv rev*q indices n-1=1 to2:
conv index 1 = rev0 q1 + rev1 q0 = b q1 + a q0
index2 = rev0 q2 + rev1 q1 = b q2 + a q1. Our out for j=0 should a0? out[0]=∑ p_i q_{0+i} = a q0 + b q1. Formula index n-1=1: b q1 + a q0 yes. g=2 cross = [2(a q0+b q1), 2(b q2+a q1)]. Derivative wrt a =2a q0+2b q1. Good.

For q from LSE, objective score=2*n*max h? h=n*c, derivative q? LSE gradient q sum1. L_h = max h + ...; derivative approx ∑ q_t h_t = n∑q_t c_t. derivative wrt p =2n cross. Good.

Now, one subtlety: LSE objective returns `score=2*L` where L approx max h. But gradient returned is derivative of L, not of 2L? We need multiply gradient by 2. Did we? We set `g_p = 2.0*n*cross`; But derivative of L with respect p: L ≈ max h = n*max c. derivative = n*2cross. Then derivative of score=2L is 2n*2cross=4n cross? Let's check. Previous original code: `score = 2.0 * n * L / (S*S)` and `g = 2.0 * conv_res` then grad = (2n)*(g/S2 - 2L/S3). Let's infer: They set L approximates max c (not 2n). conv_res = ∑ a_i q? derivative of ∑q c = 2 cross. Objective score=2n*L/S^2. Gradient wrt a = (2n/S^2) * grad_L - ... grad_L = 2 cross. So they set g=2 cross. Here L_h = max h = n max c. If we define L = hmax + logsum(beta(h-hmax))/beta (approx max h). Then score=2*L. Derivative wrt p of L = n * derivative of ∑q c = 2n cross. Derivative of score = 2 * 2n cross =4n cross. In my earlier I set g_p=2n cross, missing factor 2 for score? Let's recalc carefully.

Let c = a*a. h = n*c. L_h = logsumexp-like approx of max(h). For β→∞, L_h=max h. Score=2 L_h.
For a fixed weighted sum S_w(p)=∑ q_t h_t = n∑q_t c_t. Its derivative wrt p_j = n * 2 cross[j] =2n cross. The gradient of L_h at high β approximates gradient of weighted sum with q. So ∇_p L_h ≈ 2n cross. Then ∇_p score = 2∇L_h = 4n cross. In softmax v, grad_v = p*(g_p - mean), where g_p should be ∇_p **objective** (score). So g_p should be 4n cross, not 2n. Previous code for original with S denominator had score=2n*L/S^2; L approx max c; gradient wrt a of L is 2cross; they multiplied by 2n/S^2? They set `g = 2.0 * conv_res`, then `grad = (2n)*(g/S2 - 2L/S3)`. That includes factor 2n for the quotient but not an extra 2? Derivative of (2n L/S^2) wrt a: (2n/S^2) ∇L - (4n L/S^3) ∇S. ∇L=2cross. So first term =2n*(2cross)/S^2. They set g=2cross, grad=(2n)*(g/S2 - 2L/S3). Yes correct. In our softmax with sum1, objective score=2*n*max c /1 =2*max h. If we use L_h=approx max h, objective=2*L_h. ∇L_h=2n cross. So ∇score=4n cross. Thus set `g_p = 4.0 * n * cross`. Alternatively define L_c approx max c, score=2n*L_c, gradient =2n*2cross=4n cross. Same. So yes factor 4n. My earlier code with 2n would be half gradient; L-BFGS can handle scale but line search maybe. Use 4n.

Let's code `run_softmax_continuation` more concretely:
```
def run_softmax_continuation(p0, beta_schedule, time_limit, label=""):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p_base = normalize_p(p0)
    best_p = p_base.copy(); best_sc = internal_score(p_base)
    print(f"[{label}] initial {best_sc:.8f}")
    if len(p_base) <= 1 or time_limit <= 0:
        return best_p, best_sc
    # measure t_obj
    u, ref = init_u_ref(p_base)
    t1 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        val, _ = lse_u_objective(u, 100.0, ref)
    except Exception:
        return best_p,best_sc
    t_obj = max(time.monotonic()-t1, 1e-6)
    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        elapsed = time.monotonic()-t_start
        if elapsed >= time_limit: break
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        beta_limit = min(600.0, max(30.0, remaining / max(1, len(beta_schedule)-bi)))
        beta_end = time.monotonic() + beta_limit
        u, ref = init_u_ref(p_base)
        try:
            fun_prev, _ = lse_u_objective(u, beta, ref)
        except Exception:
            fun_prev = np.inf
        u_cur = u.copy()
        converged = False
        while time.monotonic() < beta_end:
            maxfun = int(max(100, min(2000, 2.0 / max(t_obj, 1e-6))))
            t_chunk = time.monotonic()
            try:
                res = minimize(lse_u_objective, u_cur, args=(beta, ref), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                               options={'maxiter': max(50, maxfun // 2), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-15, 'gtol': 1e-8})
            except Exception:
                break
            elap_chunk = time.monotonic() - t_chunk
            nfev = int(getattr(res, 'nfev', 1))
            if nfev > 1:
                t_obj = 0.5 * t_obj + 0.5 * (elap_chunk / float(nfev))
            x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
                break
            x_new = np.clip(x_new, -80.0, 80.0)
            fun_new = float(getattr(res, 'fun', np.nan))
            if not np.isfinite(fun_new):
                break
            if fun_new < fun_prev - 1e-12:
                u_cur = x_new
                fun_prev = fun_new
            else:
                # If the algorithm reports success, allow one final value even without fun decrease?
                if getattr(res, 'success', False) and fun_new < fun_prev + 1e-10:
                    u_cur = x_new
                    fun_prev = fun_new
                if getattr(res, 'success', False):
                    break
                # no progress; if line search failed repeatedly, break
                break
        p_beta = softmax_u(u_cur, ref)
        p_beta = normalize_p(p_beta)
        sc_beta = internal_score(p_beta)
        if sc_beta < best_sc:
            best_p = p_beta.copy(); best_sc = sc_beta
            print(f"[{label}] beta={beta:.0f} new best {sc_beta:.8f}")
        # select base for next beta
        if sc_beta < best_sc + 0.05:
            p_base = p_beta
        else:
            p_base = best_p
    # final exact from best
    return best_p, best_sc
```
Potential issue: If `fun_new` not decreasing because L-BFGS chunk started at u_cur and found no improvement, we break beta. But after a beta, p_base maybe updated if sc_beta good. If no progress at low beta, high beta may also no progress? Could be because converged. Good.

But note: Inside beta, if a chunk doesn't improve LSE fun, we break immediately; maybe line search stopped at boundary but there is improvement with smaller step? L-BFGS should find. Fine.

Maybe we should not break on no fun improvement after first chunk if maxfun small and res.success false; could try again from same u? It would repeat. Break.

`multiplicative_descent`: Need be robust if p has n=0? no.
```
def multiplicative_descent(p0, time_limit, label=""):
    if time_limit <=0: return normalize_p(p0), internal_score...
    t0=time.monotonic(); p=normalize_p(p0); best=p.copy(); best_sc=internal_score(p)
    n=len(p)
    eta=0.05
    for beta in (1000.0,5000.0,20000.0,80000.0):
       no_improve=0
       while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
           c=fft_conv(p); h=n*c; hmax=float(np.max(h)); cur_sc=2.0*hmax
           scaled=beta*(h-hmax); scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
           e=np.exp(scaled); ss=e.sum()
           if ss<=0: break
           q=e/ss
           cross=cross_corr(p,q); g=4.0*n*cross
           gmax=float(np.max(g))
           if gmax <= 1e-300: break
           # normalize g for step? Use p_new = p / (1 + (eta/g_scale)*g)? Since g often ~4. scale by gmax maybe.
           p_new = p / (1.0 + (eta / max(gmax, 1e-12)) * g)
           p_new=normalize_p(p_new)
           c_new=fft_conv(p_new); sc_new=2.0*n*float(np.max(c_new))
           if sc_new < cur_sc - 1e-13:
               p=p_new; cur_sc=sc_new
               if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                   best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur_sc; print
               eta = min(0.5, eta*1.1)
               no_improve=0
           else:
               eta *= 0.5
               no_improve +=1
               if eta < 1e-6 or no_improve > 80:
                   # increase beta or break
                   no_improve=0
                   if eta < 1e-6:
                       break
                   # reset eta for next beta?
                   eta=0.05
                   break
    return best,best_sc
```
Scaling g by gmax makes eta dimensionless; if gmax ~4, eta 0.05 corresponds 0.05 reduction relative to max. Good. If gmax very small, cannot improve.

But backtracking: if sc_new not improve, eta halves; if many no_improve, break beta and try next with eta reset. Good.

       w=np.zeros_like(h); w[idx]=1.0/K; return w
   ...

`combine_candidates`:
```
def combine_candidates(base, alt, alphas, label=""):
    base=np.asarray(base,dtype); alt=...
    if len(base)!=len(alt): alt=resample_p(alt,len(base))
    best=base.copy(); best_sc=internal_score(base)
    for a in alphas:
       p=normalize_p((1.0-a)*base + a*alt)
       sc=internal_score(p)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best=p; best_sc=sc
    return best,best_sc
```
But if base has different n than alt, resample; parent and rev same.

`resample_p`: If n_new <=1, return [1]. If n_old=1, return ones/n_new. Use linear interp.

`make_inits(n, rng)`:
```
def make_inits(n, rng):
   inits=[]
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   xn=x/(n-1) if n>1 else x
   inits.append(normalize_p(np.ones(n)))
   inits.append(normalize_p(xn + 0.01))
   inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 - xn + 0.01))
   inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*xn)))
   inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.4*np.sin(np.pi*xn)))
   inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*3*xn)))
   inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*7*xn)))
   # smooth random
   r=0.8 + 0.3*rng.standard_normal(n)
   k=max(1,n//100)
   kern=np.ones(k)/k
   rs=np.convolve(np.clip(r,0.1,None), kern, mode='same')
   inits.append(normalize_p(rs))
   inits.append(normalize_p(np.clip(0.8 + 0.4*rng.standard_normal(n),0.01,None)))
   # two block
   half=n//2
   inits.append(normalize_p(np.concatenate([np.ones(half)*1.2, np.ones(n-half)*0.6])))
   inits.append(normalize_p(np.concatenate([np.ones(half)*0.6, np.ones(n-half)*1.2])))
   return inits
```
If no parent, evaluate internal all and choose top.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(budget_s); deadline=t0+total-30.0
    print start

    p_parent=None
    try:
       raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if raw is not None:
           arr=np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
           if arr.size >= 10:
              p_parent=normalize_p(arr)
    except Exception: pass
    p_best=None; sc_best=np.inf
    if p_parent is not None:
        n_main=len(p_parent)
        print parent length, internal
        # actual eval maybe
        try:
           verified=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p_parent]))
           print verified
        except Exception: pass
        # quick combine reverse/power
        p_best = p_parent.copy(); sc_best=internal_score(p_best)
        # combine reverse
        rev=p_best[::-1]
        p_comb, sc_comb = combine_candidates(p_best, rev, [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9], label="rev")
        if sc_comb < sc_best: p_best=sc? 
        # power transforms? maybe too much; combine powers

            alt=normalize_p(np.power(p_best+1e-30, gamma))
            p_comb, sc_comb = combine_candidates(p_best, alt, [0.1,0.3,0.6], label=f"pow{gamma}")
            update
        # main continuation

        time_main = min(time_left, 0.55*total)
        p_opt, sc_opt = run_softmax_continuation(p_best, [50,200,1000,5000,20000,80000], time_main, label="main")
        update
        # multip

                   p_opt, sc_opt = run_softmax_continuation(p_start, [100,500,2000,10000,40000], time_alt*0.7, label=f"n{n_alt}")
                   update
                   if time left > 60: p_opt=mult 0.3
    else:
       # no parent
       n_main=6962
       inits=make_inits(n_main, rng)
       # score init
       scored=[]
       for idx,p in enumerate(inits): sc=internal_score(p); scored.append((sc,p))
       scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])? No lambda? Rule no lambda functions. Use `scored.sort(key=lambda...` not allowed. Can sort by manual: `scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])` is lambda. Avoid. Use list of tuples and `scored.sort()` works because tuple first element score. Good.
       topK = scored[:4]
       time_per = (deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(len(topK))
       for sc_init,p_init in topK:
          if elapsed near deadline break
          p_opt,sc_opt=run_softmax_continuation(p_init, beta_schedule, time_per*0.8, label=f"init{sc_init:.4f}")
          update
          if time left small break
          p_opt=mult time_per*0.2
          update
```

Before final, let's mentally test with n=3 uniform p=[1/3,1/3,1/3]. c=[1/9,2/9,1/3,2/9,1/9], h=3c max1, score2.
LSE beta high at hmax index2. q=[0,0,1,0,0]. cross rev_p*q: rev=[1/3,1/3,1/3], q index2; out[0]=conv[2]=? conv rev*q at index n-1=2: conv index2 = rev0 q2+rev1 q1+rev2 q0=1/3. out all 1/3. g=4n cross=12*(1/3)=4. grad_v=p*(4-4)=0. LSE stationary. For low beta, weights near center, q not exact, gradient nonzero. Good.

Now, a subtle gradient issue with LSE and q of h: L_h for max approximation is not exactly ∑q h; q=exp(β(h-hmax))/∑, L=hmax+log∑/β. Gradient of L = q * ∇h? Let's compute derivative: L = log(∑ exp(β h_t))/β after shifting? hmax term derivative can be nonzero if hmax changes due to max index? Standard log-sum-exp gradient is q∇h (not with hmax). If we use L = hmax + log∑exp(β(h-hmax))/β, derivative depends on which t is max and q, but smoothed; the formula q∇h holds for full logsumexp without shift because derivative of log∑exp(βh) = β∑q h = βL. Actually L = (1/β) log∑exp(βh); derivative = ∑q ∂h/∂p. The shifted version should have same derivative if using stable computation? The analytic derivative of shifted hmax + log∑exp(β(h-hmax))/β is not exactly q∇h because hmax term derivative includes indicator of max times (1 - average?) but the logsumexp form avoids. In numerical LSE, we should compute gradient as q * gradient of h, not including shifted max derivative. Our `q = exp_s / exp_s.sum()` and `g_p = derivative of ∑q h` should be correct for L = logsumexp(βh)/β. Our L value computed with shift equals that; gradient as q∇h correct. Good. q sums1. Great.

In multiplicative, using q weights and g_p=4n cross is true gradient (not exact max unless high β). We use it as heuristic.

def finalize_sequence(candidates, fallback):
    best_out = None; best_score = np.inf
    for p in candidates:
        if p is None: continue
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0: continue
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in arr]
        s = float(sum(out))
        if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s): continue
        scale = 1.0 / s
        out = [float(x * scale) for x in out]
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
            best_score = val; best_out = out
    if best_out is None:
        out = [float(x) for x in fallback]
        s = sum(out)
        if s <0.01: out=[0.5]*64
        else: out=[x/s for x in out]
        return out, np.inf
    return best_out, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    score = 2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2

using:
  - simplex-normalized variables p = a / sum(a), so score = 2n * max(p*p)
  - softmax parameterization of p to keep p >= 0 and sum(p)=1
  - LSE-smoothed L-BFGS continuation
  - multiplicative/backtracking polish on the exact nonsmooth objective
  - candidate blending, transformation, and occasional length exploration

The evaluator is treated as the final oracle for acceptance of the last
candidates, while fast FFT convolutions are used inside optimization.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a using FFT. Clips tiny negatives."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    mf = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, mf)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def cross_corr(p, q):
    """
    Returns array g of length len(p):
        g[j] = sum_i p[i] * q[j+i]
    where q has length 2*len(p)-1.
    This is the weighted partner-sum gradient vector for self-convolution.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(q.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    total = n + m - 1
    mf = _next_pow2(total)
    rev = p[::-1]
    R = np.fft.rfft(rev, mf)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(q, mf)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(R * Q, mf)[:total]
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def normalize_p(x):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize to sum 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.asarray([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1e300, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return x / s


def internal_score(p):
    """Fast score for p with sum 1: 2n * max(p*p)."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_conv(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 2.0
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def init_u_ref(p, ref_idx=-1):
    """
    Parameterize p in log-space with one reference coordinate fixed to 0.
    u is all log-ratios except the reference.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([], dtype=np.float64), 0
    if ref_idx < 0 or ref_idx >= n:
        ref_idx = int(np.argmax(p))
    p_ref = float(p[ref_idx])
    if not np.isfinite(p_ref) or p_ref < 1e-300:
        ref_idx = int(np.argmax(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))
        p_ref = float(p[ref_idx])
    p_ref = max(p_ref, 1e-300)

    logp = np.log(np.maximum(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), 1e-300))
    logp_ref = np.log(p_ref)
    v = logp - logp_ref

    u = np.empty(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    if ref_idx > 0:
        u[:ref_idx] = v[:ref_idx]
    if ref_idx < n - 1:
        u[ref_idx:] = v[ref_idx + 1:]

    u[~np.isfinite(u)] = 0.0
    u = np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
    return u, int(ref_idx)


def softmax_u(u, ref_idx):
    """Recover p from log-ratio parameter u and fixed reference index."""
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(u.size) + 1
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if ref_idx > 0:
        v[:ref_idx] = u[:ref_idx]
    if ref_idx < n - 1:
        v[ref_idx + 1:] = u[ref_idx:]

    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    m = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return normalize_p(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    return e / s


def lse_u_objective(u, beta, ref_idx):
    """
    LSE-smoothed score and gradient w.r.t. u for softmax parameterization.
    """
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(u.size) + 1
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0, np.asarray([], dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return 1e12, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

    p = softmax_u(u, int(ref_idx))
    c = fft_conv(p)
    h = n * c

    if h.size == 0:
        return 1e12, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

    hmax = float(np.max(h))
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e12, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = beta * (h - hmax)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        q = np.zeros_like(h, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(h))] = 1.0
        L = hmax
    else:
        L = hmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta
        q = e / s

    score = 2.0 * L

    # score = 2*max(h), h = n*(p*p).
    # d(max h)/dp ~= 2n * weighted partner-sum, so score gradient is 4n times it.
    cross = cross_corr(p, q)
    gp = 4.0 * n * cross
    gp[~np.isfinite(gp)] = 0.0

    mean_g = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad_v = p * (gp - mean_g)
    grad_v[~np.isfinite(grad_v)] = 0.0

    grad_u = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if int(ref_idx) > 0:
        grad_u[:int(ref_idx)] = grad_v[:int(ref_idx)]
    if int(ref_idx) < n - 1:
        grad_u[int(ref_idx):] = grad_v[int(ref_idx) + 1:]

    return float(score), grad_u


def run_softmax_continuation(p0, beta_schedule, time_limit, label=""):
    """
    Continue L-BFGS in log-ratio coordinates through increasing beta.
    Returns best p and its exact internal score found in the run.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p_base = normalize_p(p0)
    best_p = p_base.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(p_base)
    print(f"[{label}] start score = {best_sc:.8f}")

    n = int(p_base.size)
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    # Roughly measure cost of one LSE objective for chunk sizing.
    u, ref = init_u_ref(p_base)
    t1 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _, _ = lse_u_objective(u, 100.0, ref)
    except Exception:
        return best_p, best_sc
    t_obj = max(time.monotonic() - t1, 1e-6)

    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        beta_limit = min(600.0, max(30.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(beta_schedule) - bi))))
        beta_end = time.monotonic() + beta_limit

        u, ref = init_u_ref(p_base)
        try:
            fun_prev, _ = lse_u_objective(u, float(beta), int(ref))
        except Exception:
            fun_prev = np.inf

        u_cur = u.copy()
        chunks = 0

        while time.monotonic() < beta_end:
            if chunks == 0:
                maxfun = max(50, min(200, int(2.0 / max(t_obj, 1e-6))))
            else:
                maxfun = max(100, min(2000, int(2.0 / max(t_obj, 1e-6))))
            maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 2)

            t_chunk = time.monotonic()
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    lse_u_objective,
                    u_cur,
                    args=(float(beta), int(ref)),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxiter,
                        "maxfun": maxfun,
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-8,
                    }
                )
            except Exception:
                break

            elap = time.monotonic() - t_chunk
            nfev = int(getattr(res, "nfev", 1))
            if nfev > 1:
                t_obj = 0.5 * t_obj + 0.5 * (elap / float(nfev))

            x_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
                break
            x_new = np.clip(x_new, -80.0, 80.0)

            fun_new = float(getattr(res, "fun", np.nan))
            if not np.isfinite(fun_new):
                break

            success = bool(getattr(res, "success", False))

            if fun_new < fun_prev - 1e-12:
                u_cur = x_new
                fun_prev = fun_new
            elif success and fun_new < fun_prev + 1e-10:
                u_cur = x_new
                fun_prev = fun_new

            if success:
                break

            # If a full chunk did not decrease the LSE value, further chunks
            # from the same point are unlikely to help.
            if not (fun_new < fun_prev - 1e-12):
                break

            chunks += 1

        p_beta = softmax_u(u_cur, ref)
        p_beta = normalize_p(p_beta)
        sc_beta = internal_score(p_beta)

        if sc_beta < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_p = p_beta.copy()
            best_sc = sc_beta
            print(f"[{label}] beta={beta:.0f} new best = {sc_beta:.8f}")

        # For continuation, follow the smoothed path unless it becomes poor.
        if sc_beta < best_sc + 0.05:
            p_base = p_beta
        else:
            p_base = best_p

    return best_p, best_sc


def multiplicative_descent(p0, time_limit, label=""):
    """
    Nonsmooth polish:
      p <- p / (1 + eta * grad_score_weight), then renormalize.
    Uses LSE weights as active-peak weights and exact backtracking on score.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p = normalize_p(p0)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_sc

    print(f"[{label}] start score = {best_sc:.8f}")

    eta = 0.05
    betas = (1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0)

    for beta in betas:
        no_improve = 0
        while time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit:
            c = fft_conv(p)
            h = n * c
            hmax = float(np.max(h))
            cur_sc = 2.0 * hmax

            scaled = beta * (h - hmax)
            scaled = np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(scaled)
            s = float(np.sum(e))
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                break
            q = e / s

            cross = cross_corr(p, q)
            g = 4.0 * n * cross
            g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
            gmax = float(np.max(g))
            if gmax <= 1e-300:
                break

            # Scale by gmax so eta is an O(1) fraction of the largest step.
            p_new = p / (1.0 + (eta / max(gmax, 1e-12)) * g)
            p_new = normalize_p(p_new)

            c_new = fft_conv(p_new)
            sc_new = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c_new))

            if sc_new < cur_sc - 1e-13:
                p = p_new
                cur_sc = sc_new
                if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_sc = cur_sc
                    print(f"[{label}] multip new best = {best_sc:.8f}")
                eta = min(0.5, eta * 1.10)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                eta *= 0.5
                no_improve += 1
                if eta < 1e-8 or no_improve > 80:
                    break

    return best_p, best_sc


def combine_candidates(base, alt, alphas, label=""):
    """Try convex combinations of two normalized sequences of the same length."""
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    alt = np.asarray(alt, dtype=np.float64)
    if base.size == 0 or alt.size == 0 or base.size != alt.size:
        return base, 1e12

    best = base.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score(best)

    for a in alphas:
        a = float(a)
        if a < 0.0 or a > 1.0:
            continue
        p = normalize_p((1.0 - a) * base + a * alt)
        sc = internal_score(p)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = p
            best_sc = sc

    return best, float(best_sc)


def resample_p(p, n_new):
    """Linearly interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n_old = int(p.size)
    n_new = int(max(1, n_new))
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return normalize_p(p)
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_p(q)


def make_inits(n, rng):
    """Diverse nonnegative initializations, normalized to sum 1."""
    inits = []
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = x / float(n - 1)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    inits.append(normalize_p(xn + 0.01))
    inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 - xn + 0.01))
    inits.append(normalize_p(xn * xn + 0.01))
    inits.append(normalize_p((1.0 - xn) ** 2 + 0.01))

    inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xn)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * xn)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * xn)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 7.0 * xn)))
    inits.append(normalize_p(np.clip(1.0 + 0.30 * xn + 0.18 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 11.0 * xn), 0.01, None)))

    r = np.clip(0.8 + 0.35 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.01, None)
    k = max(1, n // 100)
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    rs = np.convolve(r, kernel, mode="same")
    inits.append(normalize_p(rs))
    inits.append(normalize_p(np.clip(0.8 + 0.45 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.01, None)))

    half = n // 2
    inits.append(normalize_p(np.concatenate([np.ones(half) * 1.25, np.ones(n - half) * 0.60])))
    inits.append(normalize_p(np.concatenate([np.ones(half) * 0.60, np.ones(n - half) * 1.25])))
    third = n // 3
    inits.append(normalize_p(np.concatenate([np.ones(third) * 0.35, np.ones(n - 2 * third) * 1.20, np.ones(third) * 0.35])))

    return inits


def finalize_sequence(candidates, fallback):
    """
    Choose final sequence by asking evaluate_sequence for a small set of
    final candidates. Falls back safely if evaluator is unavailable.
    """
    best_out = None
    best_val = np.inf

    seen = []
    for p in candidates:
        if p is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        key = int(arr.size)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(key)

        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in arr]
        s = float(sum(out))
        if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 0.01:
            continue
        scale = 1.0 / s
        out = [float(x * scale) for x in out]

        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf

        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_out = out

    if best_out is not None:
        return best_out, float(best_val)

    fb = np.asarray(fallback, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if fb.size == 0:
        fb = np.full(64, 0.5)
    fb = np.nan_to_num(fb, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    fb = np.clip(fb, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(fb))
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    else:
        out = [float(x / s) for x in fb]
    return out, np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + total - 35.0  # leave a safety margin for final evaluation

    print(f"Starting optimizer with budget {total:.1f}s")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Load previous construction if available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    p_parent = None
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 16:
                p_parent = normalize_p(arr)
    except Exception:
        p_parent = None

    p_best = None
    sc_best = np.inf

    def _update(p_new, sc_new, label):
        nonlocal p_best, sc_best
        if p_new is None or sc_new is None:
            return
        p_new = np.asarray(p_new, dtype=np.float64)
        if p_new.size == 0 or (not np.isfinite(sc_new)):
            return
        if sc_new < sc_best - 1e-14:
            p_best = normalize_p(p_new).copy()
            sc_best = float(sc_new)
            print(f"UPDATE [{label}] best = {sc_best:.8f}, n={p_best.size}")

    if p_parent is not None:
        n_main = int(p_parent.size)
        print(f"Using parent length {n_main}")

        # Optional sanity check against evaluator (once).
        try:
            parent_out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in p_parent]
            s = sum(parent_out)
            if s > 0.01 and np.isfinite(s):
                parent_eval = float(evaluate_sequence([x / s for x in parent_out]))
                print(f"Parent evaluator score = {parent_eval:.8f}, internal = {internal_score(p_parent):.8f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

        _update(p_parent, internal_score(p_parent), "parent")
        if p_best is None:
            p_best = p_parent.copy()
            sc_best = internal_score(p_best)

        # Cheap global/local symmetries and shaping.
        rev = p_best[::-1]
        p_c, sc_c = combine_candidates(p_best, rev, (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 0.90), "rev")
        _update(p_c, sc_c, "rev-blend")

        for gamma in (0.80, 1.20):
            alt = normalize_p(np.power(p_best + 1e-30, float(gamma)))
            p_c, sc_c = combine_candidates(p_best, alt, (0.10, 0.30, 0.60), f"power{gamma}")
            _update(p_c, sc_c, f"power-blend{gamma}")

        # Main continuation on best known.
        main_budget = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.55 * total)
        if main_budget > 60.0:
            p_opt, sc_opt = run_softmax_continuation(
                p_best,
                (50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0),
                float(main_budget),
                "main"
            )
            _update(p_opt, sc_opt, "main-lbfgs")

        # Exact nonsmooth multiplicative polish.
        mult_budget = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.12 * total)
        if mult_budget > 20.0 and p_best is not None:
            p_opt, sc_opt = multiplicative_descent(p_best, float(mult_budget), "multip")
            _update(p_opt, sc_opt, "multip-polish")

        # Second-round blending after polish.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline and p_best is not None:
            rev = p_best[::-1]
            p_c, sc_c = combine_candidates(p_best, rev, (0.20, 0.50, 0.80), "rev2")
            _update(p_c, sc_c, "rev-blend-2")

            for gamma in (0.90, 1.10):
                alt = normalize_p(np.power(p_best + 1e-30, float(gamma)))
                p_c, sc_c = combine_candidates(p_best, alt, (0.20, 0.50), f"pow2{gamma}")
                _update(p_c, sc_c, f"power-blend-2-{gamma}")

        # If time remains, explore a slightly larger length by resampling.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline and p_best is not None:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            alt_budget = min(rem, 0.22 * total)
            if alt_budget > 150.0:
                n_alt = int(round(float(p_best.size) * 1.25))
                if 1024 <= n_alt <= 20000:
                    p_start = resample_p(p_best, n_alt)
                    p_opt, sc_opt = run_softmax_continuation(
                        p_start,
                        (100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 40000.0),
                        float(max(60.0, 0.70 * alt_budget)),
                        f"len{n_alt}"
                    )
                    _update(p_opt, sc_opt, f"len{n_alt}-lbfgs")

                    if time.monotonic() < deadline and p_opt is not None:
                        mult_rem = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), float(0.30 * alt_budget))
                        if mult_rem > 20.0:
                            p_opt2, sc_opt2 = multiplicative_descent(p_opt, mult_rem, f"len{n_alt}-multip")
                            _update(p_opt2, sc_opt2, f"len{n_alt}-multip")
    else:
        # No parent: run a focused multi-start search.
        print("No parent found; using multistart search.")
        n_main = 6962
        inits = make_inits(n_main, rng)

        # Rank initializations by fast internal score.
        scored = []
        for idx, p in enumerate(inits):
            scored.append((internal_score(p), idx))
        scored.sort()

        p_best = inits[scored[0][1]].copy()
        sc_best = internal_score(p_best)
        print(f"Best initial score = {sc_best:.8f}")

        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        n_starts = max(1, min(5, len(inits)))
        per_start = time_left / float(n_starts)

        start_positions = scored[:n_starts]
        for _, idx in start_positions:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            p_init = inits[idx]
            budget_here = max(30.0, min(per_start, deadline - time.monotonic()))
            p_opt, sc_opt = run_softmax_continuation(
                p_init,
                (50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0),
                float(budget_here),
                f"init{idx}"
            )
            _update(p_opt, sc_opt, f"init{idx}-lbfgs")

            if time.monotonic() < deadline and p_best is not None:
                mult_rem = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), max(20.0, 0.20 * per_start))
                if mult_rem > 10.0:
                    p_opt2, sc_opt2 = multiplicative_descent(p_best, float(mult_rem), f"init{idx}-multip")
                    _update(p_opt2, sc_opt2, f"init{idx}-multip")

    if p_best is None:
        p_best = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64) / 6962.0

    p_best = normalize_p(p_best)
    print(f"Best internal score found = {internal_score(p_best):.8f}, n={p_best.size}")

    # Final candidates: best, its reflection, average, and a gently sharpened version.
    final_candidates = [p_best]
    if p_best.size > 1:
        final_candidates.append(p_best[::-1])
        final_candidates.append(normalize_p(0.5 * (p_best + p_best[::-1])))
        final_candidates.append(normalize_p(np.power(p_best + 1e-30, 0.95)))
        final_candidates.append(normalize_p(np.power(p_best + 1e-30, 1.05)))

    fallback = p_best if p_best.size > 0 else np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    out, eval_score = finalize_sequence(final_candidates, fallback)

    print(f"Final sanitized length = {len(out)}")
    try:
        final_val = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print(f"FINAL verified score = {final_val:.8f}")
    except Exception:
        print("Final verification unavailable; returning sanitized candidate.")

    return out
```
